Since Java doesn't support multiple inheritance, I can't extend both `FutureWrapper` and `CompletableFuture`. \n\nLooking at the original code, it used `implements ListenableFuture<T>` because `ListenableFuture` is an interface. Now I need a different approach.\n\nThe key insight is that in Spring 6, `CompletableFuture` IS the native future type. So the conversion doesn't really need to wrap - it can just use `CompletableFuture` directly.\n\nLet me rethink this. The ValueSource/ValueSourceFuture abstraction is the internal representation used by the library. The Spring adapter just needs to convert between `CompletableFuture` and `ValueSourceFuture`.\n\nSince `CompletableFuture` is a concrete class, the simplest approach is:\n1.
